EXISTING-CATALOG TATTOO RECOVERY AUDIT
2026-10-08 — fixed 422-image existing-catalog snapshot

Source index: .cache/visual_library_20261008/tattoo_audit/index.json
Source-index SHA-256: 91588a090002304daad7b6a96c6118c3cbef94e792e63543be9926d4361c452b

READ THIS FIRST
- All 422 indexed images were visually reviewed at contact-sheet overview scale, across all 22 sheets. This is NOT an all-images full-resolution tattoo audit.
- Audit index numbers 1–422 are contact-sheet inventory positions, NOT HD-EXP numbers or creative approval ranks. Identify sources using the exact asset ID, filename, repository path and SHA-256 together.
- Only six individual source files were additionally opened by this reviewer: indices 102, 213, 256, 260, 333 and 421. Parent separately reported eleven close-up checks; attribution is explicit in each record.
- Each image has an overview record even when no readable ink is visible. Covered skin, small knuckle marks, dark areas, backs under clothing, reflected sides and hidden wraparound lines remain unknown.
- No readable motif means none identified in this view at this scale; it does not prove the character has no tattoos elsewhere.
- Artwork family similarity does not prove exact design identity. Distinct body variants require separate source-art recovery and body-match evidence.
- Source file existence, dimensions and SHA-256 were checked for all records. Hash checks establish provenance, not visual correctness, approval or original-artist provenance.
- This fixed 422-image snapshot excludes the forthcoming fresh Grok500 drop, video frames and any uncataloged original files. Missing means not located in this indexed set, not proven absent everywhere.
- Review lanes below are audit classifications, not edits to production review decisions. No image was deleted, regenerated, promoted to Prime or externally sent.
- Side descriptions use viewer-left/right for the shown still unless explicitly character-left. Mirrored or generated images must be checked before translating to anatomical placement.
- A source tattoo cannot be faithfully recovered from covered or cropped geometry by inventing missing lines. Mark uncertainty and request the actual source or a separate reconstruction approval.

RECOVERY PRIORITIES
- Current Hon eye / know, smoking neck blaster and complete chest ship lack verified standalone originals in this indexed set.
- SHIP-SCHEMATIC-REF and SHIP-TATTOO-REF-05 are two existing flat drawings with conflicting geometry; neither has a verified exact current-body match.
- Current Lisan natural-teeth face uses AL, vertical chin GAIB, paired open-mouth creatures and eye-in-sun neck center. SH-18 schematic has a blank sun center and materially different creature geometry.
- Twenty SH JPEG body-outline schematics exist as distinct design options. They have no JPEG alpha channel and are not verified transparent/original source masters.
- Current Lisan left-arm proof exists (SH13); exact clean flat original and hidden wraparound remain unverified.
- Historical bird/cage/racer/slug/Scoundrel, literal-eye, rosette, mask, mechanical emblem, hand-card and varied ship motifs must remain separate recovery records.
- Lisan scene-generated extra hand/knuckle and chest ink is not current-spec approval. Props, jewelry, clothing embroidery, shadows and backdrop eyes are not tattoos.
- Index correction: 255 is REF-yellow-source.jpg; 256 is HD-ARMOR-SWAP-01.jpg. The four-point chest emblem belongs to 256.

CREATIVE / DELIVERY RULES
- currentRecovery: Recover existing distinct artwork and show its exact source/body match. Recovery does not approve historical or Multiverse designs for Prime.
- newCharacters: New characters may invent ink freely. In the same delivery provide a FOUR-option standalone sheet and identify which option matches the body image. Use 10–12 options only when specifically requested.
- existingCharacterChange: Keep current recovery separate from proposals. Any changed design must be labeled a proposed variant, with standalone art and a body-placement comparison.
- delivery: One clean, separately named artwork per distinct retained motif/variant; include source ID/hash, viewer/body side, placement proof, and original/reconstructed/unknown provenance. Transparent PNG or editable source is useful, but do not falsely call a body-photo crop or JPEG schematic an original stencil.
- exclusion: Multiverse and historical artifacts stay separately labeled; removal-queue assets remain provenance-only. No automatic promotion.

ACCOUNTING: 422 image records; 63 motif/variant recovery records; all 422 source hashes and dimensions checked.
This text and its JSON companion contain provenance, not attached image originals.
